Even if you are like you, I still have the nerve to say that I am a virgin?"

Both of them sighed, and there was a small movement. It could be imagined that the woman got under the eldest young man's skirt again.

It got quiet and the music stopped. I couldn't walk or sit down. If I walked, I would inevitably make a noise. I had an idea and tiptoed back to the door, making the sound of opening the door. Then I shook the key as I walked.

There was a buzz in the room, and it took half a minute for the two of them to come out. Their clothes were neatly arranged, and their hair was still messy. I pretended to be a little confused (do you think this scene is a bit idiotic? There is still that smell in the air).

The other girl turned out to be Xiaozhi's younger sister, Xiaochun. Seeing how the siblings were named, it seemed like they had to be more educated. Their father was a college student.

The eldest young master glared at Xiaochun fiercely and asked what the plans were for the afternoon. I said no, I wanted to sleep. I joked that I didn't expect there were two beauties in the room. Do you want to go together? "You have a beautiful idea!" The two beauties said in unison. I shook my head and went to the room to sleep by myself.

I didn’t think deeply about the matter, and it would be useless to think too much, including Ruomeng Yudie, who left in a few days, and not seeing each other for three months, thousands of miles away, felt like a lie, not to mention that it hadn’t really started yet.

Suddenly I felt heartache when I thought of my little girlfriend, a pain that penetrated my heart. After five or six years of relationship, couldn't I stand being curious for once? Somehow, even though it was a hot afternoon, I was shivering with cold and breaking out in a cold sweat. My heart seemed to be clutched tightly by a hand, and I couldn't breathe. I got up and took a cigarette to feel better.

We ate at Xiaozhi's house in the evening. Shaozhen had been in low spirits for two days. She had a bad appetite today and felt uncomfortable after eating a few mouthfuls of food. I wondered if it was gastroenteritis or bloating. Xiaozhi asked me if I was free tomorrow. I said it was nothing. He asked if he could take Shaozhen to see a doctor. Shaozhen said that I was not a glass person and could do it myself.

I said yes, I'll go together and let Shaozhen squeeze into the window. If you don't feel sorry for me, I feel sorry for you. Shaozhen was laughing and joking, and she was more cheerful. Xiaozhi said I wasn't busy.

Our family has a history of bruises, and our stomach is a layman's. A layman doesn't speak an expert's language, so he doesn't dare to do anything at night. He hugs Shaozhen and sleeps until dawn. As for Xiaozhi, he hugs a pillow and sleeps on the floor.

At dawn, I took Shaozhen to the Traditional Chinese Medicine Hospital. There was a sea of people there, but luckily I came. As soon as Shaozhen saw the battle, she wanted to run away, saying that she would be fine after a few days. I disagreed, but she followed me obediently.

After finally queuing, the doctor looked around and said something, which shocked the two of them.

"Young man, you are admitted to the wrong department. You should go to the obstetrics department on the third floor. Go ahead. Congratulations." Seeing that neither of us moved, he said, "Why, don't believe it. Even though I am young, I can still feel the joyful pulse accurately." The old doctor was about to retire, so he joked again: "If you don't believe it, old man, there are examinations on the first floor and B-ultrasound on the third floor. Remember to treat me when you come. Forget it this time, I passed up. I didn't have to withdraw from the wrong test. "I don't know whether it was surprise or joy, but my head was in a state of confusion. Shaozhen was calmer than me. When I went downstairs, I subconsciously took a look at the laboratory. Shaozhen hugged my arm and shook her head: "No need, let's go home." At home, Shaozhen said that she had thought about it a few days ago. Although she was not pregnant, the feeling in her body was real and she could not hide it from others.

I feel all kinds of feelings in my heart, my own seed, but it is someone else's wife, and there are so many things and reasons to mention. Shaozhen in my arms has changed from the humiliation of the past few days, and her face is full of joy and happiness.

The two of them were tired of being together like this for a long time. Xiaozhi asked back how he was doing, and saw that he didn't have any doubts or displeasure, but he was grinning happily for a long time.

It should have been thought that besides recognizing the Lord, they did not have this intention, but they did not deliberately pursue this result and just let nature take its course.

I didn't feel cheated in my heart. They didn't have children. Xiaozhi had already told me when we first met, but I just didn't think about it.

I re-examined my relationship with Xiaozhi Shaozhen. What I gave them may not be as good as what they gave me. Just like a friend on the Internet said: "I train your body, and you train my spirit." What you train is not necessarily just the body and spirit, but the deepest and most hidden part of the soul.

The next day, it may be my intention, or it may be the result of the discussion between the three of them. The three of them drove to the mountain, to a high ground. Shaozhen Xiaozhi knelt down, took out a piece of paper, and read it to the sky.

In the days to come, Xiaozhi Shaozhen will regard the children as their masters. In order not to affect their growth, this matter will be left in the dust. The children should be educated and managed. They are parents, and their obligations, responsibilities and rights are not neglected. I will be Xiaozhi’s brother and the child’s uncle.

The ceremony between the three of us was extremely solemn. Xiaozhi and I made a blood-killing alliance, but Shaozhen didn't need to. We made three copies of the oath, pressed the blood seal and burned it to the sky.

I thought it was funny afterwards. Why did you do this at that time? If there really is a day, will the sky thunder be used to kill three people who don't know what the heaven and the earth are and do evil things?

The day of departure came quickly. Lala M and Lala S came to see each other off at the airport and met the eldest son. They were old acquaintances and had a grudge (not because of me, I'm not that level. They were neighbors when they were young, and later one family prospered in business and the other became officials). The farewell almost ended in a fight. When I got on the plane, I was glad that I said goodbye to Mr. and Mrs. Xiaozhi yesterday, otherwise I would have scared him and his wife out today.

There is no airport in my city, so the plane landed in Xu Jing's city, and I happened to visit her. When I arrived at her house, I found out that her house is quite good, at least not worse than mine. Her mother was very happy for my arrival, but her father was just average. His legs were recovering well, but he was getting older and needed some time for maintenance.

Her father was a principal before retiring, and her mother was a senior teacher.

Xu Jing was as happy as a little girl, holding her arm with me and chatting non-stop, but I found her father looking at her and sighing heavily, "What's the matter?" The answer soon appeared. A man of the same age as Jing'er appeared. He was her father's former subordinate, a vice-principal. He looked good and spoke well. Jing'er greeted him, but she still held my arm and refused to let go. She was tired of me and refused to leave. The old principal winked several times and she just pretended not to know. The vice-principal was arrogant and wanted to spit fire.

Jing'er said that the vice-principal was interested in pursuing her. Her father supported her but her mother did not. However, her mother was also quite conflicted because if Jing'er's family was uneasy in the local area, they would have to leave sooner or later. It was a real dilemma.

Depending on the situation, her father won't be well for a while, and Jing'er won't be able to go back with me yet. After seeing the old man, Jing'er and she went out to chat for a while, and we could only see each other again.

Familiar city, familiar smell. The two clerks in the store suddenly saw me appearing. They couldn't react for a moment. They didn't remember to call the boss, so they screamed. It was so funny. It took a while to calm down.

I have to praise Li Hua and Luo Li. Although they are based on my previous business model, it is rare to be so organized.

When I returned to my work unit, my boss asked me what I had learned after studying for three months. I introduced the mountains and rivers and special delicacies there in great detail, and my boss was fascinated by what I had learned.

I talked for half an hour but didn't get to the point. The boss laughed and scolded you that you are just a foodie. Is it because you got lucky and stepped on shit or something else? There is news that you are also involved in this good thing. The news is true, but I don't know the reason. Are you someone above or below? I smiled bitterly and said that apart from bed, there are other things that have something to do with me, especially those that can help me. Even if it’s just a hair, how can you not be clear about it? We are called people, and we are self-aware. We know how we got to this position, but we don’t dare to think about it. Besides, at this age and level, we are at the top.

The boss said that I know, but the news is true, so it is puzzling.

I am a person who has a "sky falls", which means that if the sky falls, I will do whatever I have to do. When things get tough, I can stretch my head and shrink my head, but it is not a bad thing.

The home was quiet, spotless, comfortable and peaceful. I lay on the sofa and fell asleep soon.

I opened my eyes and saw two pairs of eyes on my face. The two elves held their chins and smiled, hugging them respectively. It was a familiar feeling and a familiar smell.

Chu Bing brought a bunch of snacks to share. When Qiu Ping prepared dinner, Qiu Ping gave me a large bowl. Chu Bing only gave one third of the bowl. Seeing me looking at it, she said, "My sister can't eat these days, so..."

"It's okay, sister, my appetite has improved today, please give me more." After saying this, she still couldn't finish eating and she still looked a little uncomfortable. Li Hua was very nervous and quickly poured a glass of water and brought it to her. Chu Bing took a sip, but she felt even more uncomfortable and ran to the sink as if she wanted to vomit.

The scene was too familiar. I asked Qiuping how long she had been there, and the answer was about ten days. I looked at the snacks on the table, which were all sour plums and spicy food. When Qiuping saw my gaze, her face suddenly changed, and her "Kang Lang" bowl fell to the ground and broke.
